# Decisions

The day-to-day decisions Alexis made with Claude, one row each, with the reason: what the ADRs
are for design, this is for the way of working. A decision taken in conversation goes here at
once (CLAUDE.md). Newest last.

| Date | Decision | Why | Where it lives |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-10-17 | Slice 1 ships as three merges: 1a the gate, 1b the app's routes, 1c the demo stack | Each under about 400 lines, so each is reviewed for real | `docs/plans/slice-1.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | `IrcMessage.command` is a branded `IrcCommand` now; typed views of commands wait for slice 4 | A bare string lets any text pass for a command | `packages/core/src/irc/irc.ts` |
| 2026-10-17 | CUBE CSS on CSS Modules, no Tailwind or SCSS; Prettier on | Compositions keep layout out of blocks; one formatter ends style debates | `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | Code, comments, commits and docs in English; Claude talks to Alexis in French and explains as to a beginner | The team reads English; Alexis wants to understand every step | `CLAUDE.md`, `CLAUDE.local.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | Coverage floor 85% everywhere and on the changed lines; core at 100% | A floor the gate holds, with no outside service | `vitest.config.ts`, `scripts/patch_coverage.py` |
| 2026-10-17 | Every review finding is reproduced before it is fixed, kept in a ledger, and the tier-1 ones go through an Opus verifier first | A claim is not evidence; Dora showed how many "confirmed" findings never ran | `docs/REVIEW.md`, `docs/review/findings.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | The harness is measured by its verified findings per merge: falling means solid, flat over three merges means simplify | Adding guards forever is a race no one wins | `docs/REVIEW.md` item 7, `docs/review-log.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | Claude Code starts in the repository, never moved there with `/cd` | `/cd` leaves the hooks pointing at the old folder and locks the session | `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md`, finding F026 |
| 2026-10-17 | The sandbox may open local ports | StrykerJS's workers, and later Playwright and the dev server, need them | `.claude/settings.json` |
| 2026-10-17 | No hook outside the sandbox runs repository code: the format hook is gone, the Stop hook only compares a recorded state | A hook runs with full access; it was a way out of the sandbox | finding F038 |
| 2026-10-17 | `qs` forced to `^6.16.0` | Three moderate advisories, reached only through StrykerJS | `pnpm-workspace.yaml`, finding F025 |
| 2026-10-17 | The dependency audit asks npm's advisory database directly (`scripts/audit.py`) | pnpm cannot run inside the sandbox | finding F027 |
| 2026-10-17 | `formatLine` sends at most 4094 bytes of tag data | IRCv3: what a client may send; 8191 is a server's limit | finding F047 |
| 2026-10-17 | Core keeps no DOM or Node types; Alexis counts UTF-8 bytes with a loop over code points | `TextEncoder` would need either, for one line of code | finding F050 |
| 2026-10-17 | The stolen-but-unbound key in `r/seal` is fixed in the seal-store slice | The fix changes the realm's storage, which that slice changes anyway | finding F057 |
| 2026-10-17 | RTK leaves `git diff`, `git show`, `git log`, `grep` and `rg` alone | It summarized a review's diff to a fifth, silently | Alexis's RTK config, finding F046 |
| 2026-10-17 | Merges and pushes are Alexis's, typed in his own shell; the GitHub token stays in the macOS keychain | The sandbox refuses a merge's writes; the keychain keeps the token unreadable to Claude | `docs/HARNESS.md`, findings F063, F064 |
| 2026-10-17 | CI on GitHub Actions, for every branch pushed; it reports, it cannot block a merge on this plan | A check outside this machine; the company's Team plan will let it block | `.github/workflows/gate.yml` |
| 2026-10-17 | No edit asks Alexis first, config files included; a new dependency, a push, `rm -rf` and chain writes still do | Too many manual approvals; the sandbox, the gate and review hold the rest | `.claude/settings.json` |
| 2026-10-17 | React's lint presets come with slice 1b; three.js gets our own skill, written from r186, in slice 7 | No external skill targets our exact stack | `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | An edit of the six files run outside the sandbox asks Alexis first: the guard, the Stop hook, `code_changed.sh`, the git hooks, `settings.json`, the `package.json` files | Edits are not sandboxed: an edit there would run with his full access | `.claude/settings.json`, finding F068 |
| 2026-10-17 | The reviewers get an exam: the ledger's reproduced findings, rerun whenever a reviewer changes; it comes after slice 1 | A reviewer is judged by the bugs it finds; Anthropic tunes its own code review this way | `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | An edit asks Alexis first again for `scripts/`, `.githooks/`, `.claude/`, the gate's configs and what pnpm runs | The git hooks run the gate with his full access when he pushes or merges: an edit there escaped the sandbox (finding F076) | `.claude/settings.json` |
| 2026-10-17 | The hooks Alexis's git runs stop running repository code; a backlog item, after slice 1 | The real fix for F076: asking before edits only narrows it | `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | In 1b, a test that kills no mutant fails the full gate (StrykerJS `disableBail`, `killedBy`) | A lint rule against `expect(1)` is easy to get around; a test that sees no change to the code is useless however it is written | `docs/plans/slice-1.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | 1b splits in two merges: 1b the app, its two pre-rendered pages, the compiler, React lint and Playwright with axe; 1b-2 the PWA, the budgets, Lighthouse and the mutant-killing check | Each under about 400 lines, so each is reviewed for real | `docs/plans/slice-1.md` |

| 2026-10-17 | The repository will be public: nothing in it names a person's machine or the demo host's services; the secret scan refuses both | Once opened, the whole history is readable | `scripts/gitleaks-private.toml` |
| 2026-10-17 | The history is rewritten once, just before the repository opens, to drop the author's email and the older commits' private names | Rewriting is irreversible and needs a forced push: once, at the end | `scripts/.gitleaksignore` |
| 2026-10-17 | `main` protected and a `CODEOWNERS` file once the Team plan is on; a backlog item | The CI can then block a merge, and the harness and configs always get Alexis's review | `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | `semver@6.3.1`, and only it, is exempt from pnpm's trust policy | It is the fix of GHSA-c2qf-rxjj-qqgw, published by npm's team before semver 6 had provenance; `@babel/core` 7, needed by React's hooks lint, requires it. It goes when nothing locked needs Babel 7: neither React's hooks lint nor the app's compiler (`@babel/core` 7 is the app's own; F107) | `pnpm-workspace.yaml` |
| 2026-10-17 | `.nvmrc` is a link to `.node-version`, so `nvm use` picks the repository's Node | His shell's default Node 26 made pnpm refuse the install; a link cannot drift from the version the gate checks | `.nvmrc` |
| 2026-10-17 | Canonical URLs use `https://gno.world`, set in one place in the app | Each page names its one address for search engines; a new domain is a one-line change | `app/src/meta.ts` |

| 2026-10-17 | Claude no longer asks Alexis before acting: it decides, records the decision here, and reports; the edit asks of `.claude/settings.json` stay | Too many upstream approvals; the asks are what closes F076, so they stay until Alexis says otherwise | `CLAUDE.md`, this file |
| 2026-10-17 | Claude builds and integrates a whole feature without asking, validated and controlled by the harness; only the six files run outside the sandbox ask again, and the hooks that run no repository code come right after 1b | Alexis wants autonomy; F076's remaining risk (what the gate runs at his push) is closed by that backlog item, not by asks | `.claude/settings.json`, `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | 1b leaves to later: Twitter Card, Open Graph images, JSON-LD, `sitemap.xml` and `robots.txt` (slice 5, with the data files); the build failing on a component the compiler cannot compile, proven by a planted case (1b-2, with the other build checks); `noindex` on the demo's URL (1c, a header at its proxy) | 1b proves the pages, their tags and the compiler on; each of these needs what its slice brings | `docs/plans/slice-1.md` |
| 2026-10-17 | The app's logic lives in `.ts` files, tested by Vitest and mutated by StrykerJS like core; its React components are walked by Playwright until slice 2 adds component tests | A real measure of the logic now, with no new dependency; the components are covered by the journeys meanwhile | `vitest.config.ts`, `scripts/patch_coverage.py` |
| 2026-10-18 | What pnpm loads asks before an edit, and pnpm runs no pnpmfile (`ignorePnpmfile`); `.claude/**` asks again | The security review: pnpm runs these with Alexis's access at every install and run (F087) | `.claude/settings.json`, `pnpm-workspace.yaml` |
| 2026-10-18 | Main takes a harness change only if a commit declares it: `Harness-changed: <path> - why` | A branch's gate is its own harness; the declaration is what Alexis reads (F089) | `.githooks/pre-push` |
| 2026-10-18 | Commit messages pass the private-name scan too | The repository will be public, messages included (F090) | `.githooks/commit-msg` |
| 2026-10-18 | Chromium runs as one process only inside Claude Code's sandbox, marked by `SANDBOX_RUNTIME`; each test has its own browser everywhere | The sandbox refuses Chromium's Mach port; elsewhere Chromium runs as it ships (F096) | `app/playwright.config.ts` |
| 2026-10-18 | Not found is a catch-all route that answers 404 in the browser; the error notices share one component, `ui/ErrorNotice` | Hydration waits for the router, so no mismatch (F091); jscpd found the copied markup | `app/src/routes/notFound.tsx` |
| 2026-10-18 | The app's build stays after jscpd and knip in the gate, held by a test, rather than adding the build to their ignore lists | One fewer ignore; the order is checked (F082, F100) | `scripts/check.sh`, `scripts/test_hooks.sh` |
| 2026-10-18 | Left for Alexis, since each edit asks: the fingerprint that changes on `git add` (F104), the semver comment (F107), `#` imports in the app's `package.json` | They touch files only Alexis's approval changes | `docs/review/findings.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | Nothing asks but a push and a chain write: the files run outside the sandbox, `pnpm add` and `rm -rf` are refused outright instead of asked, each rule naming the real files, since a deny also bans the sandbox's writes (F111) | Alexis wants no permission prompt; a refusal keeps F068 and F087 closed with none | `.claude/settings.json` |
| 2026-10-18 | Three review rounds at most per branch; after the third, a review reads only the diff since the last one | Each round found new bugs, mostly in the code the previous round's fixes added, and their severity fell: the gate and the mutation checks hold the fixes; the practice of Anthropic's own code review ("Solving code review at Anthropic", read 2026-10-17) | `docs/REVIEW.md`, step 3 |
| 2026-10-18 | F076's plan approved: outside the sandbox, the hooks run only their own lines, git and gitleaks, and check the trees the sandboxed gate recorded green | A careless or injected edit no longer runs with Alexis's access at his push; a forged record only lets a broken tree reach `main` until the CI's report | `docs/plans/harness-f076.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | A merge carries about 400 lines of product code at most, and the backlog measures DORA's five metrics on the demo | DORA's small batches: AI writes much code fast, and large changes are hard to review and test; slice 1b took three review rounds | `docs/REVIEW.md`, step 3; `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | `prepare` keeps `command -p git`; where git lives elsewhere, the hooks are set by hand | A git planted in `node_modules/.bin` never runs at install (F087); the cost, a manual step on Nix or Homebrew without Xcode's tools, is documented (F122) | `docs/DEVELOPMENT.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | A guard's tests plant its bypasses too: the comment that allows it, the tool it needs missing, the command it runs failing | Round 3 found three fixes of round 2 bypassed that way (F113, F116, F125) | `CLAUDE.md`, "How we work" |
| 2026-10-18 | Every merge runs `/simplify`, then `/code-review` and `/security-review`; the `security-reviewer` agent stays for tier 1 | The most secure and complete review with Claude Code's own commands, adding lenses and removing none (Alexis) | `.claude/commands/pre-merge.md`, step 5; `docs/REVIEW.md`, step 3 |
| 2026-10-18 | Review rounds converge as Anthropic's do: diff-only from round 2, a stop after three to reconsider the approach, a finding's class fixed whole, style-only findings dropped, `intended` and `moot` outcomes, rounds and tokens logged | Agent reviewers and authors can keep each other busy; Anthropic's pass-on-first-round rose from 65% to 78% ("Solving code review at Anthropic", read 2026-10-18) | `docs/REVIEW.md`, step 3; `.claude/commands/pre-merge.md`; `scripts/check_docs.py` |
| 2026-10-18 | The secret scan hands gitleaks every change as text instead of refusing attribute files | One fix for the whole class (F132), and a lawful `.gitattributes` no longer stops the gate | `scripts/scan_secrets.sh` |
| 2026-10-18 | The hooks live in `.git/hooks`, installed from `main` by Alexis; a merge shows the harness it changes before writing it (`merge.sh`); `prepare` goes, which ends the row on `prepare`'s git | No branch can bring hooks or settings into his shell (F129); Alexis: "corrige ça" | `docs/plans/harness-f129.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | One repository: Claude works in Alexis's folder, no separate clone; the shared `.git` is a known limit of the local hooks, and `main` protected with the CI required is the boundary, first in the backlog | Simplicity (Alexis). The review of F129 found git state the sandbox writes (`.git/commondir`, `main`, the objects); a clone of Claude's own would close it, at the cost of two folders | `docs/HARNESS.md` (Known limits), `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | The harness is frozen after F129, then made reusable elsewhere once it has proven itself on the product | It is rigorous but heavy; product merges whose harness findings fall are the proof (Alexis) | `docs/plans/backlog.md` |

| 2026-10-18 | 1c has no egress proxy: the static server sits on an `internal` network with no way out; the proxy comes with slice 3's services | Nothing in 1c calls out, and no way out at all proves more than a proxy's list; the backlog's row named the proxy for slice 1 (Alexis) | `docs/plans/slice-1c.md` §2 |

| 2026-10-18 | The static server is `nginx-unprivileged`, not static-web-server | nginx does the headers, the 404 fallback, the password file, the 429 per address and the PROXY protocol itself; static-web-server has no shell but lacks three of them (Alexis) | `docs/plans/slice-1c.md` §8 |
| 2026-10-18 | semgrep's registry rules for Dockerfile and Compose run in `/pre-merge`, fetched at run time; the gate stays frozen | Their licence forbids committing them; `docker inspect`'s read-back in `deploy/test.sh` is the proof (Alexis) | `docs/plans/slice-1c.md` §6 |

| 2026-10-18 | The demo's outbound network is a guard: a container that holds the namespace Tailscale shares, loads deny-by-default rules (only TCP 80 and 443 and UDP to public addresses; every private, link-local, carrier and connected range refused; IPv6 closed), then sleeps; it alone has `NET_ADMIN`, an exception to ADR-0002 §7's "every capability dropped"; Tailscale keeps none; `inside`'s gateway is isolated | Measured: a container on a plain network reached a service of the host, one on an internal network did not; tailscaled's proxy support is unreliable; an Opus counter-review adopted the guard with changes (Alexis) | `docs/plans/slice-1c.md` |
| 2026-10-18 | Confidentiality first: no public file says where the demo runs or who hosts it, nor any address, port, network or tailnet name; the test's output, which holds the host's addresses, is never committed | The repository will be public, and the demo's URL is public (Alexis: "rien qui puisse permettre de me hacker") | `docs/OPERATIONS.md`, the review card |

| 2026-10-19 | From 1c's review, beyond its plan: the demo compresses its files now; the host's public addresses and UDP, the order after a host restart, DNS through the router and connection limits are known limits in the runbook with a backlog row each; product files Claude writes and Alexis runs (`deploy/`, the app) stay editable, a known limit with `main` protected as the boundary; `UpdatePrompt` leaves `ui/` in slice 2 | Each is low and none breaks 1c's "done when"; fixing them now meant more code, another review and more runs (Alexis, choosing each recommendation) | `docs/OPERATIONS.md`, `docs/HARNESS.md`, `docs/plans/backlog.md` |
| 2026-10-19 | The coherence review's 38 decisions, as recommended: nine base colours with the rest computed, three meanings (selected cream, mine mint, about me amber), one label style, panel and dialog titles 28/300, an 11 px floor, a 4 px grid, radii 8/12/24/pill, controls 24/32/40/56 (44 by touch), minimum heights, three tiers of tokens, CUBE layers and compositions, closed variants and named parts, seven new components (Panel, ChoiceGroup, Progress, Tooltip, Toolbar, Logo, Combobox), one rich card for the chat, search as a ⌘K palette, a very light credits link, the browser target with two named exceptions | One token and one component for every value and element of the prototype, nothing written twice; the visible changes are shown again on before/after designs in 2a (Alexis: "go") | `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`, `docs/design/coherence.md` |

| 2026-10-19 | A safety and trust layer now: three severity levels (notice, caution amber, danger coral), a danger button, confirmation by reversibility, trust states (not encrypted, encrypted, key changed, checked by you), the network in the header with its Testnet label, and the view "Universe" renamed "Cosmos" | Mainnet, sessions and encryption build on them; the phase-0 mockups already broke ADR-0002 (no Testnet label, DMs said encrypted) (the three reviews; Alexis) | `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` |

| 2026-10-20 | A person's country shows as its flag, a picture from one set, not the two-letter code nor an emoji | Warmer than the code; emoji flags break the no-emoji rule and Chrome and Edge on Windows draw two letters instead; the set (about 250 drawings) is a dependency chosen with its licence when messages are built (Alexis) | `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md` |
